koṟki, pāṇṭiyap pēraraciṉ paḻamperum kōnakaram, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ paṇpu vāynta tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉam; iṭic caṅkam nilaviya mūtūr; perum pulami vāynta pulavarkaḷum pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷum oruṅkiruntu muttamiḻ vaḷartta iṭic caṅkam nilaviya pērūr. iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē āyiram āyiram kal toliviṟku appāliruntu kirēkkarkaḷum, yavaṉarkaḷum urōmarkaḷum cāvakarkaḷum ciṅkaḷavarkaḷum pīṉiciyarkaḷum ekiptiyarkaḷum papilōṉiyarkaḷum vaṇikattiṉ poruṭṭup periya marakkalaṅkaḷil vantiṟaṅkiya pāṇṭiya maṉṉar kaḷiṉ akanakarāyum puṟanakarāyum koḷḷappaṭṭa mānakarākum. potuvākat tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkum ciṟappākap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṟkum vāḻvaḷitta koṟki muttuk koḻitta poṟpuṟum puṉita nakaramākum. iṅkētāṉ ayalnāṭṭiliruntu kōṭi kōṭiyākap poṟkācukaḷum poṟpāḷaṅkaḷum vantu kuvintaṉa. koṟki poṉkoḻikkum poṟpuṟum puṉitamāṉa paṭṭiṉam ākum.
iṅkup pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ, vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyeṉa, talimuṟiyākac celvap perukkuṭaṉ vāḻntu vantaṉar. koṟki maṉṉarkaḷiṉ māḷiki aṟputamāṉa aḻakoḷirum viṇmuṭṭum māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiyatāy viḷaṅkiyatu. iṅkup pala araṇmaṉi kaḷum, upparikikaḷum uyyāṉavaṉaṅkaḷum kōṭṭi kottaḷaṅ kaḷum kaṭaṟpaṭiyum, tarippaṭiyum, tērppaṭiyum, parippaṭi yum, karippaṭiyum, viṟpaṭiyum, vēlpaṭiyum, iruntu vantaṉa.
koṟkiyil paḻampāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ āṭci ki.mu.500 mutal ki.pi. 300 vari iruntirukkalām eṉṟu varalāṟṟup pulavar cilar varintuḷḷārkaḷ. appāl talinakar maturikku māṟṟap paṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl nuṇṇaṟivu vāynta varalāṟṟu āyvu mikka aṟiñarkaḷ ki.mu.3 allatu 4 āvatu nūṟṟāṇṭilē pāṇṭiyar kōnakaram maturikku māṟṟappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.1 maturiyil talinakaram kaṭṭap paṭṭuk koṇṭirukkum poḻutu pāṇṭiya maṉṉar maturi arukil uḷḷa maṇalūr allatu kaliyāṇapuram eṉṉum ūril iruntaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. vaṭapāṇṭiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa maturi kōnakarākac ciṟappup peṟṟeḻuntālum teṉ pāṇṭi nāṭum koṟkiyum pāṇṭiyarkaḷukkup puṉita pūmiyāka iruntuvantaṉa. koṟki iraṇṭāvatu talinakarāka nīṇṭa kālam perumip paṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. maṉṉar maturiyilum maṉṉaṉiṉ tampiyō, makaṉō iḷavaracaṉāy koṟkik kōmāṉ eṉṉum paṭṭat tuṭaṉ koṟkiyilum iruntu varuvāṉ. iḷavaracaṉ koṟkiyil iruntu kaṭal vaṇikattik kavaṉittu varuvāṉ.
koṟkip paṭṭiṉattil iṭic caṅkam nilaviya kālattil - atāvatu talinakar nāṉmāṭak kūṭalukku māṟṟappaṭumuṉ vāḻnta koṟki maṉṉarkaḷ aimpattoṉpatu pēr eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum; eḻupattoṉpatu pēr eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷil iṟuti yil vāḻnta pāṇṭiyaṉ muṭattirumāṟaṉ eṉpavaṉ āvāṉ. koṟkik kōnakaram eṉṟa peyar māṟṟappaṭṭu, kūṭalukku - (maturi) kōnakaram eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatum koṟkippaṭṭiṉam ciṟappiḻantatu. āṉāl, aracaṉ kūṭalukkup pōyviṭṭālum iḷavaracaṉ koṟkiyilē iruntu pāṇṭiya aracukkut tuṇi yāka iruntu vantāṉ. iḷavaracarkaḷ, koṟkik kōmāṉ, koṟki iḷavaracar eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭi vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ aracaṉ iṟantāl iṅkiruntu maturi pōy aracu kaṭṭilil ēṟi maṇimuṭiyum ceṅkōlum tāṅkuvar. koṟkiyāḷi, koṟki vēntaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭamum cūṭik koḷvar.
āṉāl, koṟki kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉamāy iruntatu. kaṭal koṟkiyiṉiṉṟu nāṉku kal toliviṟkup piṉtaḷḷip pōkavē, koṟkiyil, kappalkaḷ vantu niṟkum vacatiyi kuṉṟiyatu ataṉāl tuṟimukam eṉṉum peyari iḻakka nēriṭṭatu tuṟi mukam paḻiya kāyalukku māṟṟappeṟṟatu. koṟki, eñciyirunta oru ciṟu perumiyiyum iḻantatu. maturiyil pāṇṭiyap pēraracu vīḻntatum pāṇṭiya iḷavaracarkaḷ teṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṟkut tirumpiṉar. koṟkiyilum, tirunelvēliyilum teṉkāciyilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum iruntu kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷāka vāḻntu vantaṉar. ki.pi. 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil pāṇṭiyap pēraracu maturiyil nilikulintatu. āṉi iruntu aracāṇṭa iṭattil pūṉi iruntu pulampi aḻutatu pōla muculimkaḷum, nāyakkarkaḷum āṅkilēyarkaḷum māṟi māṟi āṭciyik kippaṟṟi makkaḷi aṭimikaḷākki curaṇṭi vāḻntu vantaṉar.
māṟavarmaṉ kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya makkaḷ aracuk kaṭṭilukkākac caṇṭiyiṭṭaṉar. tilli cultāṉ utaviyip pāṇṭiyaṉ makaṉ oruvaṉ nāṭiṉāṉ. cultāṉiṉ paṭivīraṉ mālikkapūr perumpaṭiyuṭaṉ vantu nāṭṭic cūṟiyāṭip pāṇṭiya araca kumārarkaḷi aṭittu viraṭṭi nāṭṭiluḷḷa poṉṉiyum maṇiyiyum koḷḷiyaṭittu ṭilli cultāṉukku aṉuppiviṭṭu aracu kaṭṭilil ēṟikkoṇṭāṉ. ki.pi. 1330 mutal 1378 vari maturiyil muculim āṭci eḻuntatu. appāl muculim āṭciyāḷarkaḷi nāyakkarkaḷ viraṭṭi viṭṭu aracukaṭṭilik kippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. iṟutiyāka āṅkila aracu āṭcipīṭat til amarntatu 1947il intiyā viṭutali peṟṟatu; pāṇṭiya nāṭu - iṉṟu intiya āṭcikkuḷ iruntuvarukiṟatu.
ki.pi. patiṉāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉ piṟpakuti vari teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭil pāṇṭiya araca kulattil uḷḷa cilar ciṟṟaracarkaḷ pōl āṅkāṅku āṭcipurintu vantuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ nelli teṉkāci. karivalamvantanallūr, koṟki mutaliya ūrkaḷil iruntu koṇṭu āṭci naṭatti vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ koṟkip pāṇṭiyaṉ. tirunelvēlip pāṇṭiyaṉ, teṉkācip pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṟu aḻikkap paṭṭu vantaṉar. tiruccentūr māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa cāttaṉkuḷattilkūṭa oru pāṇṭiyaṉ iruntu vantatākak kūṟappaṭuvatu uṇṭu.
pāṇṭiyap pēraracu nilikulintu uṭintu citaṟuṇṭu pōyiṟṟu. pāṇṭiya iḷavaracarkaḷ koṟkiyit talimip patiyāka vittukkoṇṭu cila ciṟṟūrkaḷil cila iḷavaracarkaḷ iruntu āṭci naṭatti vantaṉar.1 ivarkaḷ nāyakka maṉṉarkaḷukkut tiṟi celutti vantaṉar. ciṟu pāḷiyakkārarkaḷ pōl vāḻntu vāḻkki naṭattiṉar. eṉṟālum mīṇṭum citaṟuṇṭa pāṇṭiyap pēraracik kaṭṭi uyartti viṭalām eṉṟu kaṉavu kaṇṭaṉar. cilar, ataṟkāka viṇava camayattit taḻuvit tirumāli vēṇṭi niṉṟaṉar. cilar pārppaṉak kurumārkaḷiṉ coṟpaṭi yākam iyaṟṟi cōmāciyār eṉṉum paṭṭam tāṅki kiyil irunta kācukaḷiyum pārppaṉarkaḷukkup pātakāṇikki vittu vaṇaṅkiyum payaṉiṉṟi vaṟumiyāl vāṭi maṟintaṉar. cilar maṉivi makkaḷ aṇintuḷḷa nakikaḷi viṟṟuk kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭi āṭciyip peṟa aḻutu toḻutu naṭip piṇaṅkaḷāyt tirintalintu maṟintaṉar iṟutiyāka pāṇṭiyar kuṭumpam peyar colla nātiyaṟṟup pōy viṭṭatu. iṉṟu ativīrarāmapāṇṭiyaṉ vaḻivantavarkaḷuḷ nāṉ eṉṟu koṭi vaḻippaṭṭiyal kāṭṭi urimi koṇṭāṭa evarum varakvaṭiya niliyilli. eṉavē inta nāṭṭil vāḻum ellā iṉattavarkaḷum tāṅkaḷē pāṇṭiya kuṭumpattiṉar eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum nilimiyil iruntu varukiṟatu. itu mika veṭkak kēṭākum.
caṅkak kāla ātip pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ palar aṟa neṟipaṟṟi yoḻukum āṉṟa neṟiyuṭiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉar. piṉṉar tōṉṟiya palar aṟaneṟi vaḻuvi maṟaneṟi taḻuvi vantuḷḷaṉar. uṟṟāriṭamum māṟṟāriṭamum pōril īvu irakkamiṉṟi naṭantu vantaṉar. caṅkak kālap pāṇṭiyaruḷ mikap pukaḻpeṟṟa palyāka cāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉpōṉ pōril puliyeṉa niṟpavaṉ. pulipōl pakivarkaḷ mītu pāyum maṟakkulap pāṇṭiyaṉ pakivarkaḷik kaṇṭatum mārtaṭṭi, cavāl viṭṭu cimmakkural eḻuppip paṭikkaḷam pukuvāṉ. avaṉatu pulikkural kēṭṭup pakivarkaḷ kilikoṇṭu vīḻvar. avaṉ noṭip poḻutil pakivarkaḷ mītu pāyntu veṭṭi vīḻttip puṟaṅkāṭṭiyōṭum paṭi ceytuvarum valimi vāyntavaṉ. avaṉ muṉvitta kāli, piṉviyāta vīramaṟavaṉ; tōlviyē aṟiyāta tolkuṭit tōṉṟal;  veṟṟi muracoliyit tavira vēṟu oliyi avaṉ cevi maṭuttatē illi.
ivaṉ pakivarkaḷiṭam oru ciṟitum irakkaṅkāṭṭātavaṉ. pōril pakivarkaḷ tōlviyuṟṟāl avaṉ paki aracaṉiṉ maṉivi makkaḷic ciṟippiṭittu varuvāṉ. pakivaṉ taliyil muṭicūṭac ceytu taṉmuṉ maṇṭiyiṭṭu amarac ceytu taṉ kālāl avaṉ maṇi muṭiyi iṭaṟu vāṉ. tōlviyuṟṟa maṉṉaṉ taliyi moṭṭi yaṭittu avaṉ talimītu, cuṟṟilum cāṇiyi vittu naṭuvil eṇṇey ūṟṟi, tiriyiṭṭu viḷakkēṟṟi aracaṉi naṭiviḷakkākkit taṉ araṇmaṉi vāyilil niṟkac ceyvāṉ. avaṉ araṇmaṉiyi iṭittu vīḻtti, kaḻuti pūṭṭiya ēriṉāl uḻutu karukkum kurukkum erukkum vitippāṉ. ikkoṭuñ ceyal piṟakālat tamiḻ aracar cilariṭamum iruntatu. itu tamiḻakat tiṟkē oru cāpakkēṭāka muṭintatu!
koṟkiyil irunta iḷavaracarkaḷē, maturiyil maṉṉarkaḷ kālamāṉatum araca kaṭṭili aṭipavarkaḷāka iruppar. cilappatikāram kūṟum neṭuñceḻiyaṉ iṟantatum avaṉ tampi koṟkiyāḷi eṉṉum paṭṭan tāṅkiya iḷavaracaṉ veṟṟivēṟ ceḻiyaṉ maturi ceṉṟu maṇi muṭi cūṭi ceṅkōl ēnti, aracu kaṭṭilil amarntāṉ.
pāṇṭiyap pēraracu uṭintapiṉ teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭil vāḻntu vanta pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṟ cilaratu peyarkaḷum avarkaḷ vāḻnta kālaṅkaḷum namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi aṭiyil varumāṟu :
1. 	parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ I 	ki.pi. 1387
2. 	parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ II 	ki.pi. 1384-1415
3. 	parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ III 	ki.pi. 1401-1435
4. 	caṭiyavarmaṉ kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1393
5. 	caṭiyavarmaṉ vikkiramapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1401-1422
6. 	caṭiyavarmaṉ parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1422-1463
7. 	arikēcari parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1422-1461
8. 	caṭiyavarmaṉ kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1429-1473
9. 	aḻakaṉperumāḷ parākkirama pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1473-1506
10. 	caṭiyavarmaṉ cīvallapaṉ 	1534-1543
11. 	caṭiyavarmaṉ parākkiramakulacēkaraṉ 	1543-1552
12. 	nelvēli māṟaṉ 	1552-1564
13. 	caṭiyavarmaṉ ativīrarāmapāṇṭiyaṉ 	1564-1604
14. 	varatuṅkarāma pāṇṭiyaṉ 	1588
15. 	varakuṇarāma kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉ 
	(kulacēkara cōmāciyār) 	1613
16.	varakuṇarāma pāṇṭiya 
	kulacēkaratēva tīṭcitar 	1748
pāṇṭiya marapiṉarkaḷiṉ āṭci ki.pi. 1748 ām āṇṭu vari talitūkki vantuḷḷatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. iyēcu capiyic cērnta kattōlikkak kurumārkaḷiṉ kuṟippuppaṭi pāṇṭiyar āṭci ki.pi. 1523 mutal 1666 vari nilavip paṭippaṭiyāy aḻintu maṟintatu eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. ki.pi. 1756-l kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi maturi, tirunelvēlic cīmikaḷik kippaṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar. āṅkāṅkē cila kulatturōkikaḷi aḻittu maṉṉar cākirtār, camīṉtār eṉṟum paṭṭam aḷittu makkaḷi āṭciyāḷarkaḷukku aṭimippaṭutti, vari vacūl ceytu, vēṇṭiyavikaḷiyellām eṭuttukkoṇṭu taṅkaḷukkum ciṟitu koṭukkumpaṭi ēṟpāṭu ceytu koṇṭaṉar. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ camīṉtārkaḷ evarāyirup piṉum avarkaḷ aṉivarum inta nāṭṭi aṭimippaṭutti innāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ varippaṇattil vāḻntuvanta pakivarkaḷiṉ kiyāṭkaḷē eṉpati nāṭu eṉṟum maṟappataṟkilli.
koṟki, paḻam pāṇṭiyarkaḷ vāḻnta mūtūr, iṭiccaṅka mum eṇṇaṟṟa pulavarkaḷum kūṭi tamiḻāynta nallūr. pāṇṭiyap pēraraciṉ paṇpumikka iṇik kayalkaḷ poṟitta ceṅkoṭi paṟanta pērūr. kirēkkarkaḷum yavaṉarkaḷum urōmarkaḷum arāpiyarkaḷum piṟarum maṉam uvantu vantu kuṭiyēṟiya kaṭinakar. ulakil uḷḷa palvēṟu nāṭṭu marakkalaṉkaḷum namnāṭṭu nāvāy kaḷum ampikaḷum vaṅkaṅkaḷum vaṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum pōkku varattiṉ poruṭṭum naṅkūram pāyccit tattam koṭi paṟakka niṉṟa māperum paṭṭiṉam, tamiḻakattiṉ ceyporuḷkaḷiyum viḷi poruḷkaḷiyum ciṟappāka muttukkaḷiyum piṟa nāṭṭiṟku viṟṟuk kōṭi kōṭiyākap poṟkācukaḷiyum, taṅkap pāḷaṅkaḷiyum peṟṟut tamiḻakattip poṟkaḷañciyamākkiya mānakar.
iṉṟu anta mānakar - koṟki, cīr aḻintu ciṟappaḻintu oru ciṟu ūrāka, paṭṭikkāṭāka tēṭuvāraṟṟuk kiṭakkiṟatu. ataṉ perumiyi mīṇṭum uyarttuvatu tamiḻarkkum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkum ciṟapput taruvatākum. eṉavē tamiḻmakkaḷ kiḷarnteḻuntu koṟkiyi maṟumalarcci peṟac ceyyavēṇṭum. aṅku oru kallūri toṭaṅka vēṇṭum. toḻiṟcāli niṟuvavēṇṭum pōkkuvarattu vacatikaḷip perukkavēṇṭum. putiporuḷ āyvut tuṟiyiṉar, atika akkariyeṭuttu akaḻ āyvu naṭatta vēṇṭum. tūttukkuṭit tuṟimukam koṟkit tuṟimukam eṉappeyar cūṭṭappeṟa vēṇṭum. muṭiyāviṭil oru mīṉ piṭittuṟimukamākavāvatu viḷaṅkavēṇṭum. koṟkippaṭṭiṉam. mīṇṭum nalla nākarikamāṉa nakaramāka maṟumalarcci peṟa aracāṅkam āvaṉaceyya vēṇṭum. itaṟku namatu māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ellāc caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷum amiccarum tuṇi puriya vēṇṭum. aṅku oru cuṟṟulā māḷikiyi eḻuppalām. tolporuḷ kāṭci cāliyi niṟuvalām.

14. paḻiya kāyalum pāṅkum
cīrvikuṇṭam vaṭṭattil uḷḷa cāyarpurattiṟkuk kīḻpuṟam mūṉṟu kal tolivilum tūttukkuṭi - tiruccentūr neṭuñ cāliyil - kaṭaṟkariyil paḻiya kāyal eṉṟoru ciṟṟūr iṉṟum uḷḷatu. ivvūril iṉṟum vāḻum makkaḷiṟ perum pālōr parata kulattavarē yāvar.
patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ pukaḻ peṟṟa koṟkit tuṟimukam cīrkeṭṭup pōyiṟṟu. eṉavē paḻiyakāyal kaṭaṟ pakutiyip pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ tuṟimuka mākap payaṉpaṭutta muṟpaṭṭaṉar. paḻiyakāyal pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉamāy ciṟappuṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu kūṟa eṇṇaṟṟa nalla cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṅku akaḻntu pārkkum poḻutu arēpiyar, cīṉar, urōmar nāṭṭuk kācukaḷum kōppikaḷum pīṅkāṉkaḷ, matuppuṭṭikaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. ciṟanta intiya varalāṟṟup pērāciriyar viṉceṉṟu cimītu, kāyalip paṟṟi, tam varalāṟṟu nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
koṟkiyil uḷḷa vaṇikat toṭarpuṭiya ceyalkaḷ aṉittum āṟṟiṟkuk kīḻpuṟam - mūṉṟu kaltolivil uḷḷa kāyalil putitāka niṟuvap peṟṟa tuṟimukattiṟku māṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. kāyal pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ vari pāṇṭiya nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟṟuṟi yākavum pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭāvatu talinakaramākavum kīḻnāṭṭu māperum vaṇikat talimip patikaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum naṉṟākavum viḷaṅki vantatu. patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil mārkkōpōlō eṉum ittāliya vaṇikar iṅkē tāṉ kappal ēṟiṉār. avar iṅku oru muṟi aṉṟip pala muṟi vantu pōyirukkiṟār. muṭi maṉṉarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, kuṭimakkaḷum eytap peṟṟa celvap ciṟappiyum puṟakkōla mēṉmiyiyum vanta vaḻippōkkarāṉa avar kaṇṭukaḷittār; viyantu pārāṭṭi ṉār. atit tam nāḷ kuṟippil eḻutiyum vittār. koṟkip paṭṭiṉam pāḻpaṭak kāraṇamāka irunta atē nikaḻcciyē kāyal tuṟimukamum kiviṭap paṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka cīrkeṭṭu amintatu.1
aṅkumīṉ piṭittu vāḻum cila paratakula mīṉavarkaḷum oru cila muculimkaḷum kiṟittava camayan taḻuviya cilarum oṉṟiraṇṭu intukkaḷum cila kuṭicikaḷum eñciyuḷḷaṉa.
paḻiya kāyal
paḻiya kāyal, kāyal eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭum. koṟkiyip pōl kāyalum māṭamāḷikikaḷ niṟinta māperum tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉamāy nilaviyatu. āṉāl kāyalukku ipperumi nīṇṭa kālam nilittirukkavilli. cila āṇṭukaḷil tuṟimukam tūrntu pōyviṭṭatu. iṅkiruntu koṟkiyip pōl muttum miḷakum paṭṭum naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷum ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
paṭṭu yāṇṭiruntu varukiṟatu eṉṟum atu evvāṟu ceyyappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟum urōmarkaḷ aṟiyārkaḷ. atu oru vakip pañcu eṉṟum ētō oru cila maraṅkaḷil paṭippumuṟi tōṟṟuvitta ōr iḷañciṟaku eṉṟum nīṇṭakālam eṇṇi vantaṉar. koṟkiyil muttum pavaḷamum eḷitākak kiṭittaṉa. muttu vaṇikam iṉṟikkum mutaṉmi peṟṟē viḷaṅkukiṟatu. kīḻnāṭṭiṉarum mēṉāṭṭiṉarum muttukkuḷitta matippē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. oru muttu maṭṭum rūpāy mūvāyiram vilipōtal aritāka nikaḻvataṉṟu - eṉṟu aṟiñar tōrmalē kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
koṟkiyip pōṉṟu kāyalum muttukkaḷi ēṟṟumati ceytu pukaḻ īṭṭivantatu.
mārkkō pōlō eṉṉum ittāliya nāṭṭu vaḻip pōkkar intiyā pōnta poḻutu atāvatu ki.pi. 1295-ām āṇ ṭilē koṟkit tuṟimukam tūrntu kāyal tuṟimukam eḻuntu viṭṭatu. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ putiya tuṟimukamākiya kāyalilē mārkkōpōlō marakkalam ēṟiṉār eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. avar tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil taṇporuni āṟṟu mukattiliruntu oṉṟarik kal tolivil uḷḷa paḻiya kāyalpaṭṭiṉam māperum vaṇikac ciṟappu vāynta tākavum nirvākat tuṟikku ēṟṟa iṭamākavum miḷirkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēlum, itu pāṇṭiyar kaḷiṉ perum kaṭal vāṇikattiṟku oru miyamākavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē pāṇṭiyarkaḷ innakarip pēṇi vaḷarttup pukaḻpeṟac ceytuḷḷaṉar eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṉṉum avar mēṟku nāṭukaḷiliruntu periya kalaṅkaḷ ellām kāyal tuṟimukatti aṇukāmal pōvatilli eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. atōṭu avaratu kālattil uḷḷa pāracīka varalāṟṟāciriyar vācapu ali (Wassaf Ali) intiyā, cīṉā nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷ aṉittum kāyalukku vantu aṅkiruntu mēli nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup paḻiya kāyal eṉṉum pāṅkāṉa tuṟimukam intiya nāṭṭiṟkut tiṟavu kōl pōṉṟa iṭattil amintiruntatu. ittuṟimukatti liruntē cēra nāṭṭu miḷakum, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu muttum, karunāṭaka nāṭṭuc cantaṉak kaṭṭikaḷum, piṟanāṭṭut tantam, kōtumi, avuri, karuvāppaṭṭi pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷum ēṟṟumati ceyyap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟār.
pāṇṭiyap pēraracar paḻiya kāyalil uḷḷa araṇmaṉiyil amarntiruntār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. paḻiya kāyal pakkatti luḷḷa kaṭalil viliyuyarnta naṉ muttukkaḷ atikam kiṭittaṉa. mēlum 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattiliruntu kāyal tuṟi mukattil yavaṉartam eṇṇaṟṟa marakkalaṅkaḷum, cōṉakarkaḷiṉ nāvāykaḷum vantu kuḻumi niṉṟaṉa. perumaḷavil aṅku vāṇikam naṭipeṟṟatu.
paḻiya kāyalil, taṇ poruni, kaṭalōṭu kalakku miṭattil oru kōyil uḷḷatu. pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ poruni kaṭalōṭu caṅkamam ākum iṭattil kuḷittu viṭṭu arukil uḷḷa caṅkumuka īcuvarar kōyilil ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. ik kōyilil paḻiya kalveṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
araṇmaṉi
paḻiya kāyal paṭṭiṉattukkuk kiḻakkē pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ vāḻnta araṇmaṉikaḷum, kōṭṭi kottaḷaṅkaḷum iruntu aḻintu pōyiṉa. iṉṟu ataṉ aṭittaḷaṅkaḷum iṭipāṭukaḷumē kāṇakkiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
veḷināṭṭup paṇṭaṅkaḷ
cīṉa nāṭṭu uṭinta pīṅkāṉ taṭṭukaḷum, cāṭikaḷum, paḻiya kācukaḷum iṅku, iṉṟum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku āyirak kaṇakkāṉa  kutirikaḷ veḷināṭukaḷiliruntu kappal mūlam iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ivvūril 5000 - kku mēṟpaṭṭa uṟi kiṇaṟukaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum avikaḷik kutirikaḷ kuḷip pāṭṭavum, kuṭinīreṭukkavum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. iṉṟu inta uṟikkiṇaṟukaḷ cilavaṟṟit tōṇṭit tūreṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. iṅku, akaḻntu pārkkum poḻutu eṇṇaṟṟa maṉitarkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ivi iṅku naṭipeṟṟa pōril uyirnītta vīrarkaḷiṉ elumpu kaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. paḻiya kāyaliṉ vaṭapakkamuḷḷa irāmaccantira purattil perum maram eṉṟu oru maratti aḻikkiṟārkaḷ. immaratti namatu nāṭṭil vēṟu eṅkum kāṇamuṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟalām. itu ātirēliyāvil uḷḷa maram eṉṟu aṟiñar kālṭuvel kūṟukiṟār. pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuḷ varum vaḻakku kaḷit tīrkka im marattaṭiyil ceṉṟu cattiyam ceyvārkaḷām. āṉāl iṉṟu appaṭi etuvum naṭappatilli.
kaṭṭapommaṉ ciṉṉam
kaṭṭapommaṉ kālattuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cilavum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuk kaṭṭapommaṉ kaṭṭiya piḷḷiyār kōvilum, ataṉmuṉ oru nakarā maṇṭapamum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itupōl tirucentūriliruntu pāñcālaṅkuṟicci vari pala nakarāmaṇṭapaṅkaḷ iruntaṉavām. (nakarā - muracu) tiruccentūr murukaṉ kōvilil naṭuppakal pūciyiṉpōtu aṭikkappaṭum nakarā aṭuttaṭuttuḷḷa nakarā maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ paṇiyāḷarkaḷ mūlam toṭarntu aṭikkappaṭṭu, pāñcālaṅ kuṟicci vari nakarā oli eṭṭum, in nakarā oliyik kēṭṭatum kaṭṭapommaṉ pāñ cālaṅkuṟicciyil uḷḷa murukaṉ tiruuruvi vaḻipaṭṭup piṉ uṇavu uṇpāṉām!
kāyalil kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kōṭṭiyi nirmāṇittavar maṉṉar cūrapatma rājā eṉṟum, ivarē iṟutiyāka vāḻnta pāṇṭiya aracar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ippeyar nāyakka maṉṉar peyar pōlat terikiṟatu. cilar itu mukamatiya maṉṉar peyar eṉ kiṉṟaṉar. itu tavaṟu. kāyalil uḷḷa mīṉāṭci kōvilil cūrrācā viṉāl aḷikkappaṭṭa oru ceppēṭu uḷḷatu. atu iṉṉum paṭikkappaṭavilli. atu akkōvil pūcāriyiṭam uḷḷatu. atu paṭikkappaṭṭāl cila uṇmikaḷ veḷippaṭalām. iṅkuḷḷa cūraṅkāṭu eṉṉum ciṟṟūr cūr aracaṉ peyarāl tōṉṟiya ūr eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kutiri vaṇikam ceyyavanta arāpiyar ivvūrukkarukē kuṭiyēṟiṉar. avvūrē iṉṟu kāyalpaṭṭiṉam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvūril iṉṟuḷḷa makkaḷiṟ perumpālōr arapikaḷiṉ cantatiyiṉar eṉṟē kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum marakkalaṅ kaḷil vantatāl marakkalarāyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ic collē marikkāyar eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. kīḻakkari, nākappaṭṭiṉam pōṉṟa kaṭaṟkarikaḷil vāḻum muculīmkaḷum marikkāyar eṉṟē aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
mārkkōpōlō
aṟiñar iyūl (Colonel yule), tam, mārkkōpōlō eṉṉum nūlil kuṟippiṭuvatāvatu kāyal eṉṉum pāṇṭiyar kaḷiṉ kaṭaṟṟuṟiyum paṭṭiṉamumākiya iṭam aḻivuṟṟup pal lāṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarē aṟiñarkaḷāl kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. kāyal ceṉṉi mānilattil, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa kaṭaṟkariyil nīṇṭakālamākap pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ tuṟimuka māka viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpatu makkaḷ uḷḷattiṉiṉṟum maṟantu pōyviṭṭatu. intap paṭṭiṉam paṟṟi ki.pi. 1292-l mārkkō pōlōvāl viḷakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. naṭu ūḻiyil intiyāviṉ kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkariyil oru kaṭaṟṟuṟiyākavum mika mukkiyamāṉa nakaramāyum viḷaṅkiya ivvūr potuvāka aṇṭi yil vāḻum makkaḷāl paḻiya kāyal eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭu vantatu. itu tāmiravaruṇi āṟṟiṟku aṇmiyil, ataṉ āṟṟu mukattiṟku oṉṟarik kaltolivil iruntatu eṉṟu nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tirunelvēli varalāṟṟi varinta aṟiñar muṉivar irāparṭṭu kālṭuvel avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.1
mārkkōpōlō iṅku vantiruntapōtu kāyalil kaṇṭavi avaratu kavaṉatti īrttaṉa. kāyal perum paṭṭiṉamāka vum ciṟanta nakaramākavum maṉṉariṉ aintu kumārarkaḷil mūtta makaṉāṉa acar (Ashar) eṉpavarukku urittāṉatākavum iruntatu. ōrṉacu, kicu (pāracīka vaḷikuṭāvil uḷḷa oru tīvu) ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta kappalkaḷellām intat tuṟimukattil tam naṅkūrattip pāycci niṉṟukoṇṭiruntaṉa. aṉṟiyum aṭīy (Adeei), arēpiyā, ilēṭaṉ mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṉṟu vanta marakkalaṅkaḷ viṟpaṉikkākat tam nāṭṭuk kutirikaḷiyum piṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu vantu iṟakkik koṇṭiruntaṉa. itu, anta ūrilum atic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa makkaḷi oṉṟu tiraḷumpaṭi ceykiṟatu. eṉavē kāyal eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattil perum vaṇikam naṭipeṟṟu vantatu. aracariṭam ērāḷamāṉa celvam kuvintu kiṭantatu. aracaṉ viliyuyarnta maṇikaḷ iḻikkappeṟṟa poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷit taṉ kātilum, kaḻut tilum, kikaḷilum, iṭuppilum aṇintiruntāṉ. avaṉ māperum aracik kavaṉamākak kāttu naṭatti varukiṟāṉ. avaṉatu āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa nāṭu naṉku iyaṅki varukiṟatu aṅku nītiyum, nērmiyum, oppuravup paṇpum uṇṭu. vaṇikarkaḷukkum, ayal nāṭṭiṉarkkum nalla ātaravum, aṉpum, calukiyum aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ivaṉuṭiya nakarip pārppataṟku nāṉ mikavum makiḻcci aṭikiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mārkkōpōlō kālattil intiyā pōnta pāracīka varalāṟṟāciriyar vācapu māpāriṉ tuṟimukam kāyal eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ. eṉṟu kūṟi māpārip paṟṟik kūṟiyirukkiṟār. ciṉ, mecciṉ eṉṉum ariya nakaraṅkaḷiliruntu (cīṉāvil vaṭakkut, teṟkup pakutikaḷiluḷḷa ūrkaḷ) vanta mali pōṉṟa caṅ eṉṟa kappalkaḷ intu, cintu, lēṭaṉ mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu aḻakiya poruṭkaḷi ēṟṟikkoṇṭu kāṟṟiṉ iṟakkikaḷik koṇṭu nīriṉmītu paṟantu vantu āṅku eppoḻutum varuvatum, pōvatu māka irukkiṉṟaṉa. pāracīka vaḷi kuṭāviṉ celvaṅkaḷ, kuṟippāka īrākku, kurōcāṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu, ēṉiya nāṭukaḷi aṇi ceyyum poruṭkaḷ, māpāriliruntu airōppāviṟkum urōmiṟkum celvataṟku vaḻiyāka paḻiya kāyal intiyāviṉ tiṟavukōlāka irukkiṟatu1 eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ayalār ātikkam
14ām nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭakkak kālattilē tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku ēḻari nāṭṭuc caṉiyaṉ piṭittuviṭṭatu. mūṉṟu tamiḻ maṉṉar kaḷum nili kuliya muṟpaṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. ki.pi. 1327il ṭilli aracaṉ mukammatu piṉ tukḷak māpār nāṭṭit taṉ kālaṭiyil koṇṭu vantāṉ. ki.pi. 1335il calāl uttīṉ acaṉ eṉpavaṉi araca paripālaṉāka niyamittāṉ. āṉāl avaṉ maturiyil taṉiyāṭci niṟuvikkoṇṭāṉ. 1340 kiyācu uttīṉ maturi maṉṉaṉākit tamiḻakam muḻuvatiyum accuṟuttiṉāṉ. 1378 - vari muculim āṭci naṭi peṟṟatu.
appāl vicayanakara nāyakkarkaḷ, muculim āṭciyi akaṟṟit taṅkaḷ āṭciyi niṟuviṉar. 16ām, nūṟṟāṇṭil vicuvanāta nāyakkaṉ (1529-64) āṭci tirucciyiliruntu kumarimuṉi varip paraviyatu. 1565-ām āṇṭil maturiyil tirumali nāyakkaṉ āṭci ceyta kālattil iyēcu capip pātiri mārkaḷ maturiyil vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. ki.pi.1675il makārāṭṭirar āṭci taḷirttatu. civāciyiṉ tampi veṅkāci pōṉcalē 1676il tañciyil nāyakkar āṭciyi oḻittu aracu kaṭṭilēṟiṉāṉ. 1498il pōrccukkīciya mālumivācu kōṭakāmā marakkalattiṉ mūlam āppirikkāvic cuṟṟivantu kōḻikkōṭṭi (kaḷḷik kōṭṭi)yil kāl vittāṉ. akkālattē paḻiya kāyalil muttuk kuḷikkum toḻil paratavarkaḷiṭamiruntu muculimkaḷiṭam pōyviṭṭatu. ki.pi. 1514il ittuṟimukattiṟku pōrccukkīciyak kappaliṉ kappittāṉ (Captin) pār pōcā varumpoḻutu kāyaliṉ muttuk kuḷikkum toḻil kollattil uḷḷa oru aracaṉukku uriyatāy iruntatu. 1542ām āṇṭu tūya cavēriyār muttuk kuḷikkum tuṟiyi mutaṉmutalākap pārviyiṭa vantār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. appoḻutu paṭṭiṉam muṟṟilum pōrccukkīciyar kikkup pōyviṭṭatu.
paḻiya kāyaliṉ pakkattē kāyalpaṭṭiṉam, mañcaḷ nīrk kāyal, puṉṉikkāyal vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ paṭṭiṉam māṟaṉmaṅkalam eṉṟu pala ūrkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. puṉṉik kāyal akkālattil paratavarkaḷukku oru nalla kuṭiyēṟṟap pakutiyāy iruntatu. 1552-ām āṇṭil puṉṉikkāyal oru ciṟu kaṭaṟṟuṟiyākak kūṭa iruntatu. iṅku oru maṇṇālāṉa kōṭṭiyum eḻuppap peṟ ṟiruntatu. akkōṭṭi pōrccukkīciyak kappittāṉ kōvārṭ ṭiṉō ātikkattiṉkīḻ iruntatu. ivaṉ paṭakarkaḷāl tōṟkaṭik kappaṭṭāṉ, eṉṟālum pōrccukkīciyar paṭi tiraṭṭi vantu mīṇṭum kōṭṭiyik kippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar.
mutal accakam
puṉṉik kāyalil pōrccukkīciyar taṅkaḷ araciyal ātikkatti nilināṭṭa camayatti nilināṭṭi ataṉ mūlam araciyal ātikkatti uṟutippaṭuttat tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉar puṉṉik kāyalil oru accakatti niṟuvi atil tuṇṭut tāḷkaḷ acciṭṭu vantaṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷukku kiṟittava camayattip pōtikka oru tamiḻ nūli veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. annūl puṉṉik kāyal accakattil 1578ām āṇṭu, aṭiyār varalāṟu (Flos Sanctrum) eṉṟa peyarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. tamiḻil acciṭappaṭṭa nūṟkaḷil itu mūṉṟāvatu nūl eṉṟeṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ maṟupatippu ip poḻutu pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi tūya cavēriyār kallūrit tamiḻt tuṟit talivar tavattiru. muṉivar irācamāṇikkam aṭika ḷāriṉ perum muyaṟciyāl tūttukkuṭi tamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻaka veḷiyīṭāka veḷivantuḷḷatu.1 iḵtaṉṟi irumaruntakamum, urōmaṉ kattōlikkac camaya pōtaṉik kūṭamum (Seminary) kōṭṭik kāvaṟ paṭiyum iruntaṉa. 1596-ām āṇṭu kāyalil mīṉ piṭikkum tuṟiyiṉ taḷapatiyāy oruvar paḻiya kāyalil iruntu vantār.
iṭoṉ cepattiyar eṉpavar, kiṟittavap paratavarkaḷ muttukkuḷittu muttu eṭuttu vantāl atil pattil oru paṅku kiṟittavakkōyil makamikkuk koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu oru vitiyi ēṟpaṭuttiṉār. tamiḻakattil muculim āṭci arumpiya poḻutu muculimkaḷum muttuk kuḷikkavum mīṉ piṭikkavum muṟ paṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṉmiyāṉa iṉattavar kaḷāṉa paratavarkaḷukkum muculimkaḷukkum mōtalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl aṉṟiyap pāṇṭiyanāṭṭu āṭciyāḷar muculim āka iruntatāl tamiḻnāṭṭup paratarkaḷ ātaravu iṉṟit tavittaṉar; avarkaḷ muculimkaḷiṭam tōlviyuṟṟu vantaṉar. pāṇṭiya nāṭu pōnta pōrccukkīciyar paratavarkaḷukku ātaravu kāṭṭiyatōṭu kattōlikka camayattilum avarkaḷ iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytu viṭṭaṉar. appāl pōrccukkīciyar kattōlik paratavarkaḷukku nalla araṇ pōṉṟiruntaṉar. nāyakka aracarkaḷāl muculim āṭci tamiḻakattil maṟiyavē, kattōlikkap paratavarkaḷ kaṭal toḻilit taṅkaḷ kālaṭiyil vittuk koṇṭaṉar. muculimkaḷ at toḻili ṉiṉṟu peritum vilakik koṇṭaṉar. itaṉ palaṉākap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa kaṭaṟṟuṟikaḷil vāḻnta paḻantamiḻk kuṭikaḷāṉa paratavarkaḷ aṉivarum kattōlikka kiṟittavarkaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉar. āṉāl tañci, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum ceṉṉiyilum uḷḷa paratavarkaḷiṟ cilar intukkaḷākavē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. ki.pi. 1605il ulāntakkārarkaḷ, vaṇikam eṉṉum pērāl intiyā pōntaṉar. 1658il ulāntakkārarkaḷ pōrccukkīci yarip puṉṉik kāyaliṉiṉṟu viraṭṭi aṭittatuṭaṉ kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkariyiṉiṉṟum ōṭumpaṭi ceytaṉar. ulāntakkārarkaḷ maturi maṉṉaṉiṭamiruntu muttuk kuḷippu, caṅkuk kuḷippu, mīṉ piṭipput toḻilkaḷiṉ muḻu urimiyiyum peṟṟaṉar. 1664il intiyāviṟkup pireñcukkārarkaḷ vantaṉar.1 avarkaḷ kattōlikkap paratavarkaḷukku peritum ātaravaḷittu vantaṉar. 1795il āṅkilēyar kikku āṭci māṟum vari tamiḻakak kaṭal ātikkam ulāntakkārarkaḷiṭamē iruntu vantatu. itaṉāl 1825il kiṟittava camayappaṇi pīṭuṟṟatu.
koṟkiyiṉ poṟpuṟum tuṟimukam 12-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil kaṭalkōḷāl maṇmēṭiṭṭut tuṟimukattiṟkuriya vacatiyi iḻantatu. ataṉ piṉ, paḻiya kāyal pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ tuṟimukamāka eḻuntatu. ïj‹ fhy« ek¡F¤ â£lt£ltkhf¡ TwKoaÉšiy v‹whY« ï¤jhÈa eh£L tÊ¥ ngh¡f® kh®¡nfhnghnyh 13M« ü‰wh©o‹ ïWâÆš - m~jhtJ 1292ïš fhaš JiwKf¤â‰F tªJ f¥gš V¿dh® v‹W bjÇ»wJ.* koṟkit tuṟimukam ki naḻuvappaṭavirunta atē kaṭalkōḷ, paḻiya kāyal tuṟimukamum kinaḻuva viṭappaṭuvataṟ kum kāraṇamāyt tōṉṟiyatu. ataṉāl pōrccukkīciyar 1580-ām āṇṭil tūttukkuṭiyil kuṭiyēṟi atit tuṟimukam ākkiṉar. puṉṉik kāyaliyum avarkaḷ taṅkum iṭamāka ākkik koṇṭa ṉar. 1658ām āṇṭil ulāntakkārarkaḷ (Dutch) tūttukkuṭit tuṟimukattik kippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. 1782-ām āṇṭil ulāntak kārarkaḷiṭamiruntu tūttukkuṭit tuṟimukattik kiḻak kintiyak kampeṉiyār 1782ām āṇṭil paṟittuk koṇṭaṉar. mīṇṭum 1785ām āṇṭil ulāntakkārarkaḷ kikkup pōy 1796ām āṇṭil kampeṉiyār āṭcikku vantatu. mīṇṭum 1818ām āṇṭu pepruvarit tiṅkaḷ 9ām nāḷ ulāntākkārarkaḷ kikkup pōyiṟṟu. piṉṉart tūttukkuṭit tuṟimukam 1825ām āṇṭu cūṉ tiṅkaḷ 1ām nāḷ amitiyāy - camātāṉattuṭaṉ - āṅkila āṭciyiṉ kikkup pōyiṟṟu.  iṟutiyāy 1947ām āṇṭu ākattut tiṅkaḷ 15ām nāḷ āṅkilēyar, intiyarka ḷiṭam mika amitiyāy tūttukkuṭit tuṟimukattiyum - ēṉ? intiyāviyum intiyarkaḷiṭam oppuvittuviṭṭu iṅkilān tiṟkuk kappal ēṟik kaṭuki akaṉṟuviṭṭaṉar.1
mārkkōpōlō tamatu nūlil teṉṉintiyāvip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvatāvatu,2 māpār nāṭṭiṉ māperum mānilattil pāṇṭiyanāṭu perumi vāyntatu. iṅkuḷḷa nāṭukaḷ aṉitti ṉum ciṟappu vāyntatu. iṅku aintu uṭaṉpiṟantārkaḷāṉa aracarkaḷ āṭci purikiṟārkaḷ. inta mānilam ulakilēyē uyarnta paṇpu vāyntatum, nērttiyāṉatumākum. itaṉi āṭcipuriyum aracarkaḷil cuntarapāṇṭiyat tēvar eṉpavar muṭicūṭiya maṉṉar āvar. avaruṭiya āṭciyil arumperum aḻakiya muttukkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
kāyal periyatum aḻakiyatumāṉa paṭṭiṉam. ataṉuṭiya paḻam perumiyum niṉivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum iṉṉum āynta kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭavilli. iṉṟiyak kāyalukku vaṭakkē mūṉṟu, nāṉku kal tolivil māṟamaṅkalam (māṟōkkam) eṉṉum ūrukku oṉṟarik kal aḷavil uḷḷa camaveḷiyil uṭinta pāṉi caṭṭikaḷum, paḻaṅkācukaḷum, piṟaporuṭkaḷum kiṭit tiruppatu mārkkōpōlōviṉ aṟikkiyi uṟutippaṭuttu kiṟatu. kāyal ōr oppaṟṟa kaṭaṟṟuṟi eṉṟu muculim varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷ varivati iṅkuk kiṭikkum cīṉa, arāpiya nāṭṭup pīṅkāṉkaḷum, caṭṭikaḷum, poṉ, veḷḷi, ceppuk kācukaḷum uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
kāyalil vāḻum toṉmi vāynta tirāviṭap peruṅ kuṭiyic cērnta tamiḻp paratavarkaḷ ēṟattāḻa 30-āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, āṅkila āṭci nilavum kālattil kāyaliṉ paḻam perumiyum, ariya ciṟappukkaḷumākiyavaṟṟi eṭuttukkāṭṭik kāyal tuṟimukatti maṟumalarcci aṭiyacceyya muyaṉṟaṉar. tūttukkuṭiyi viṭak kāyal periya tuṟimukamāvataṟkuriya ellā vāyppiyum peṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉṟu aracāṅkattiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. āṉāl aṉṟu āṭcip pīṭattil amarntirunta āṅkila āṭciyāḷar ceviyil avarkaḷ vārtti aṇuvaḷavum pukavilli. kāyal makkaḷ attuṭaṉ toṭarntu kiḷarcci ceyya villi. cōrntu iruntu viṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷiṭam makkaḷ palamum illi; pōtiya celvamum illi.
nēru āṭciyil tūttukkuṭiyip periya tuṟimukamākka muṉvantaṉar. atu pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatēyākum. āṉāl tūttuk kuṭit tuṟimukam koṟkit tuṟimukam allatu kāyal tuṟi mukam eṉṟa peyar peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. varalāṟṟu rītiyākap pārttāl koṟkit tuṟimukam eṉṟu peyariṭap peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu naṭuvaṇ aracāṅkattiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭi irukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl tamiḻar varalāṟṟic cemmiyāka aṟiyāta cilar tamiḻaka araciṉ talimip pīṭattil iruntatāl atu naṭipeṟavilli!
eṉṟālum, iṉṟu, tamiḻakattil tamiḻaracu taḷirt tuḷḷatu. tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ varalāṟṟiyum tamiḻap paṇpāṭṭi yum pēṇa muṉvantuḷḷa iṉṟiya tamiḻaracu iti naṭuvaṇ aracukku eṭuttuc colla vēṇṭum. itaṟkākak koṟki makkaḷum, ciṟappāka tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa makkaḷum, potu vākat tamiḻakamum āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum.
āṟṟiṉāl eḻunta pirivukaḷ
arāpi nāṭṭiliruntu vantu kuṭiyēṟa virumpiya makkaḷukkup puvic cakkaravartti ceyavīrarācu kāruvāl koṭukkappaṭṭa paṭṭayattil pala ūrkaḷ cērntutāṉ kāyal eṉṟu aḻikkap paṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. mēlum taṇporuniyāṟu ūṭaṟuttuc ceṉṟu pala kiḷikaḷākap pirintataṉāl periya nakar pirintu, muḷḷakkāṭu, kuliyaṅ karical, pullāveḷi, kōvaṅkāṭu, aṭikkalapuram, mañcaḷ nīrkkāyal, akaram, māṟa maṅkalam, koṭuṅ kaṇi, kuruvittuṟi, cērnta pūmaṅkalam, puṉṉikkāyal, kīṟaṉūr, āvaraṅkāṭu, canta ecuttēvu, kurucaṭi, kāyal paṭṭiṉam eṉṟa peyarkaḷāl pala ciṟṟūrkaḷāyiṉa.
nāṭṭuppāṭal
paḻiya kāyalil peṇ makkaḷum kuḻantikaḷum pāṭivarum nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ cila uḷa :
pāṭip pāṭit taṇṇīr eṭukkum pāṇṭiya nakarkāyal
ūriccuṟṟip puḷiyamaram uluppi viṭṭāl kalakalakkum
pēric cuṟṟik kūppiṭuṅkaḷ pērāṉa kāyalūri
caṇṭi piṭikkum mukkāṇi; cāṭci collum māṟamaṅkalam
maṇṭipōṭṭu vaḻakkut tīrkkum makarācaṉ kāyalūr.
eṉap palappāṭal iṉṟum pāṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟappu
arc, cavēriyāriṉ intiyamicaṉ eṉṟa nūlil paḻami mikka (St Francis Xaviers Indian Mission) paḻiya kāyaliṉ ciṟappu cīrum pala uḷa.
paḻami
pāṇṭiya aracu ki.mu. 600 - 700 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ ceḻip puṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu mekacutaṉicu eṉṟu cantira kuptaṉiṉ aviyil irunta kirēkkat tūtuvar kūṟuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.1
paṟipōṉa celvaṅkaḷ
tillic cultāṉiṉ taḷapati cēykak camāluttiṉ, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu vantu ati veṉṟu 700 erumikaḷ cumakkattakka koḷḷip poruḷkaḷi 1373ām āṇṭil tillikku aṉuppi vittuḷḷāṉ. 80āṇṭu toṭarntu koḷḷi yaṭikkappaṭṭu vantatāl nāṭu avaṉāl vaṟumik kaṭalil mūḻkiyatu eṉṟālum vicayanakara aracaṉ kālattil paḻiya kāyal peritum paviciḻantatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.2
paḻiya kāyal pakutikaḷukku māṟōkam, pāval, kāval, kāpil, māpar eṉṟu pala peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅki vantatāka mārkkō pōlō kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tiṟavukōl
uvāciṅ eṉṟa pāracīka varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ cīṉā, cintu mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu mukkiyamāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum, viḷiporuṭ kaḷiyum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu varum ayal nāṭṭuk kappalkaḷ kāyalukku vantuviṭṭup piṉ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāracīkam, carāka, kurācam, ram mutaliya nāṭukaḷ, muṉṉāl atika celvam peṟṟiruntataṟkuk kāraṇam intiyā viṟkut tiṟavukōl pōlirunta kāyalē eṉṟu mārkkōpō lō kūṟiṉār eṉṟu muṉivar kālṭuvel tirunelvēli varalāṟṟil (pakkam 28) kūṟiyuḷḷār.
muttu
peyṉ nāṭṭut tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyil mikap parumaṉāṉa muttu oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. atu koṟkikkum, kāyalukkum arukil uḷḷa kaṭalil eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
vācukōṭi kāmā, 1498il mutaṉ mutalāka intiyāvik kaṇṭa airōppiyar eṉṟu cila varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. atu mikattavaṟu, ataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē pāṇṭiyarkaḷ urōmip pēraracuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭu vaṇikam vaḷarttuḷḷaṉar. vācukōṭikāmā intiyā pōnta poḻutu kāyalil uḷḷavarkaḷ pala muttumālikaḷik kaḻuttil aṇintu koṇṭiruntatākak kūṟukiṉṟār. appoḻutu muttuk kuḷippatu oru muculim aracaṉ kiyil iruntatu. 1658il muculim kaḷi ulāntak kārarkaḷ kārmaṇal eṉṟa (Coromandal Coast) muttuk kuḷittuṟiyiṉiṉṟu viraṭṭiviṭṭu, kāyalilum piṟa iṭaṅ kaḷilum paṇṭakacālikaḷi niṟuviṉar. avarkaḷ appoḻutu maturiyil āṭci purinta maṉṉariṭamiruntu muttuk kuḷikkavum caṅkukaḷ eṭukkavum urimi peṟṟaṉar. mīṉ piṭikka aṉumati peṟṟatiṉāl avarkaḷukku atikamāṉa varuvāy vantatu. 1795ām āṇṭu toṭaṅki iṟutiyāy 1825ām āṇṭu vari āṅki lēyarkaḷ perum muyaṟci ceytu nāṭṭiṉ ātikkam aṉitti yum kippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar.1
kāyalil, pāṇṭiyar, cōḻar, cērar, pallavar, teluṅkar, kaṉṉaṭar, cīṉar, parmiyar, arāpiyar, kirēkkar, urōmar pōṉṟa pala nāṭṭavarkaḷiṉ kācukaḷum, kōppikaḷum, cāṭikaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
tūya. tōmiyār iṅkē kariyēṟiyataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka iṅku oru ciṟu kōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu puṉṉik kāyalukku vaṭapuṟam irukkiṟatu.
ki.mu. 302ām āṇṭu ciṉa camayattiṉar iṅku pōntaṉar. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ uyirōmpum neṟiyi makkaḷukkup pōtittu vantuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iṅku iruntaṉa. iṉṟum pala camaṇac cilikaḷ iṅku uḷḷaṉa.
iṉṟum iṅku caṅku makēcuvarar kōyilum tirumāl kōyi lum iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷukku mēṟkē aḻivuṟṟuk kiṭakkum kōyilkaḷ muculimkaḷiṉ paṭiyeṭuppāl aḻikkap paṭṭavi.
1543ām āṇṭil tūya cavēriyārāl iṅku oru kattō likkak kōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ulāntākkārarkaḷāl oru piriviṉik kiṟittavak kōyilum kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. alā vutīṉiṉ paṭittalivaṉ, aṭakkappaṭṭa oru kōriyum, alāvutīṉ paṇiyāṭkaḷil oruvaṉ aṭakkappaṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭum 40-kiyūpik aṭinīḷa muḷḷa kallaṟiyum maṟṟumoru muculim kōriyum kāppirik cāmi kōyilil vaṇaṅkappaṭum āppirikka nāṭṭuc cili oṉṟum iṅku uḷḷaṉa.1

15. kāyalpaṭṭiṉam
taṇporuni āṟu kaṭalōṭu kalakkum iṭattil āṟṟiṟku vaṭapāl koṟkip paṭṭiṉam iruntatu. āṟṟiṟkut teṟkē āttūrukku aṇmiyil - teṉpuṟam kāyalpaṭṭiṉam amin tuḷḷatu. iṉṟu ivvūr tirunelvēli - tiruccentūr iruppup pātiyil irukkiṟatu.
9ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷukku arāpiya nāṭṭiliruntu cila arāpiya vaṇikarkaḷ kutirikaḷi marakkalattil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu vantu koṟki tuṟimukattil iṟakki viṟpaṉi ceytu vantaṉar. pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷukku āṇṭutōṟum arāpi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu 10,000 kutirikaḷ iṟakkumati yāyiṉa. mēlum tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu iñci, miḷaku, nīlam mutaliya vaṟṟi vāṅkit tam kappalil arāpi vaṇikarkaḷ ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉar. ivvaṇikap perukkāl pala arāpiya vaṇikarkaḷum vaṇikap piratinitikaḷum, avarkaḷiṉ paṇiyāṭkaḷum koṟkikku arukē taṇporuni āṟṟiṉ teṉpāl kaṭaṟkariyil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅ kiṉar. avarkaḷ innāṭṭiṟku marakkalattil ēṟivantatāl marakkala rāyar, marakkāyar, marikkār eṉṟellām aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ vāḻum iṭam cōṉakar paṭṭiṉam, cōṉakar cēri eṉṟum, āṟṟiṟku vaṭakkē veḷḷiyar kuṭiyēṟiya iṭam veḷḷiyaṉ irukki, yavaṉaccēri eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. cōṉakarkaḷ tam peṇkaḷi aḻittu varavilli. taṉṉantaṉiya rāka vantu tamiḻnāṭṭup peṇkaḷiyē maṇantu koṇṭaṉar. ākiyāl avarkaḷiṉ tāymoḻi tamiḻāyiṟṟu.
vaṇikam vaḷara vaḷara arāpiyarkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttu vantu tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. iṉṟu avarkaḷ vāḻum iṭam kāyal paṭṭiṉam, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṭam 12000 - arāpitiṉārukku vāṅkappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu.
arāpiyarkaḷ kutiri vaḷarppati naṉku terintavarkaḷ; kutiri ilakkaṇamum naṉku uṇarntavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ oru kutiri yiṉ vili 100-mārkku veḷḷiyiṉ peṟumatikkuk kūṭutalāṉa 500-cakkip poṉ vili ākum. kutirikaḷ vāṅkuvatil pāṇṭiyarkaḷ, nāṭṭiṉ celvattiṉ perum pakutiyi vīṇākki vantaṉar. avarkaḷ vāṅkum kutirikaḷil pala uyiriḻantu vantaṉa. avi kaḷi eppaṭi vaḷarppatu, eṉṉa tīṉi aḷippatu eṉpatu avar kaḷukkut teriyātu eṉṟellām mārkkapōlō kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl, pāṇṭiyarkaḷukkuk kutiri ilakkaṇam naṉku teriyum; kutirikaḷi vaḷarkkavum naṉku teriyum eṉpatu tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇam, acuva parīṭci mutaliya nūlkaḷiṉ mūlam naṉkaṟiya muṭikiṟatu.
kutiri viṟka vantavarkaḷ aṉṟi vēṟu pala arāpiyarkaḷum iṅku vantu kuṭiyēṟa muṟpaṭṭaṉar. ki.pi. 13āvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil, ceyyat camāluttīṉ eṉṉum periyār talimiyil arāpi muculimkaḷ palar kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. tiru.ceyyatu camāluttīṉ, periyār mukammat napi avarkaḷiṉ 21-āvatu talimuṟiyil vantavarkaḷ. ekittil kalipā koṭuṅkōl āṭci purintār. mēlum aṅkup pūmi atircciyum veḷḷap perukkum eḻuntaṉa. eṉavē aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷiṟ cilar ki.pi. 1284 paḻiya kāyal tuṟimukattil vantu iṟaṅkik kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. appoḻutu cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ āṭci naṭipeṟṟatu. varalāṟṟu āciriyar caṭavarmaṉ iraṇṭām cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ nāṭṭi āṇṭu vantāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ āṭciyil kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil vāḻnta mukammatu tīpiyiṉ makaṉ takiyyuttīṉ aptul rakumāṉ pāṇṭiya maṉṉariṉ piratinitiyāyum ālōcakarāyum iruntār eṉṟu kūṟap paṭukiṟatu. kāyalpaṭṭiṉattil kiṭittuḷḷa arāpik kalveṭṭiṉ paṭi pala muculimkaḷukku nayiṉār eṉṟa paṭṭam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa tākat terikiṟatu. cēkkāli nayiṉārāṉa ceṇpaka mutaliyār akamatu nayiṉārāṉa vīrapāṇṭiya mutaliyār mutaliya paṭṭaṅ kaḷip pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ muculimkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. nayiṉār, ūriṉ nāṭṭāṇmikkārar āvar. avar vaḻakkukaḷi vicārittut tīrppuc ceyvār. avar paḷḷi vāyilukku varum poḻutu paṇiyāḷar avarukku araca kuṭi piṭittuk koṇṭu varuvārkaḷ. paṭṭattu nayiṉārkaḷukkuc ciṟappāṉa āṭikaḷ uṇṭu.
ki.pi. 1553il pāṇṭiya maṉṉar muculimkaḷ cilarukku aḷitta paṭṭayam iṅkiruppatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. pāṇṭiyar kaḷiṉ iṟutik kālattilum appāl maturi nāyakka maṉṉar āṭciyilum iṅkuḷḷa muculimkaḷ ciṟappāṉa calukikaḷ vantu peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.
iṅku 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa arāpi nāṇayaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. iṅku akaḻntu kaṇṭa cila poṟ kācukaḷ ceṉṉit tolporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil uḷḷaṉa.

16. ātittanallūrum aḻipāṭukaḷum
ātittanallūr paṟampu eṉpatu tirunelvēli - tiruccentūr neṭuñcāliyil uḷḷa oru mēṭṭu nilam. itu pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭiyiliruntu 11-kal tol tolivil taṇ poruni āṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyil uḷḷatu. ivviṭattil iṉṟu ūr etuvum illi. āṉāl makkaḷ ippakutiyiṉi āticcanallūrp paṟampu eṉṟu aḻikkiṟārkaḷ. ippaṟampil makkaḷ vāḻntataṟkuriya aṟikuṟikaḷ etuvum kiṭikkavilli. pakkattil periya nakaram etuvum iruntirukkalām. annakaramakkaḷ itip putikuḻiyāka payaṉpaṭutti irukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
mutaṉmutalāka 1876ām āṇṭu cermaṉ nāṭṭiluḷḷa perliṉ nakariccērnta muṉivar cākar1 eṉpavar iṅkup pōntu akaḻ āyvu naṭattiṉār. atil, pala īmattāḻikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.  ivi mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷ (mutumattaṉ tāḻi) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. ittāḻikaḷil pala elumpukkūṭukaḷum poṉ, irumpu pōṉṟa vaṟṟāl ceyyappeṟṟa pala poruḷkaḷum pala aḻakiya maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa. avi aṉittum perviṉ tolporuḷ kāṭcic cāliyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
mīṇṭum iṅkē pirañcu nāṭṭiluḷḷa pārīcu nakarattic cārnta em. lūyi lēppikkiyū eṉṉum aṟiñarāl 1903 il akaḻ āyvu naṭattap peṟṟatu. 
mēlum pala cuṭumaṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa tāḻikaḷum maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷum, irumpiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa paṭikkalaṅkaḷum, taṅkattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷum, pittaḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷum kaltirikikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ittu ninta tuṇikaḷiṉ pakutikaḷum arici, varaku mutaliya kūlapporuḷkaḷiṉ umikaḷum tāḻiyiṉuḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iṅku atikamāṉa civappu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. pittaḷip pāttiraṅkaḷum irumpāl ceyyap peṟṟa viḷakkukaḷumākap pala poruḷkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ vaṉi vatil vallavarkaḷāy iruntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ulōka vēlip pāṭṭilum necavut toḻililum, taccut toḻililum kūṭa ciṟantiruntaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum maṇ veṭṭi, koḻu mutaliyavikaḷiṉ mūlam, iṅku nilaviyatu, āṟṟaṅkarip paṇpāṭu eṉṟum ivarkaḷatu mukkiya toḻil payirt toḻil eṉṟum terikiṉṟaṉa. pittaḷiyil vilaṅkukaḷ, pūcceṭikaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷi urukki vārttiruppatu avarkaḷiṉ kittoḻiṟ ciṟappik kāṭṭukiṟatu. arappā makkaḷip pōl ātittanallūr makkaḷum timil perutta kāḷic ciṉṉamum nakara nākarikamum peṟṟa makkaḷ eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.
aṟiñar em. lāppikkiyū avarkaḷiṉ, 1903-1904 ām āṇṭiya āyvil, ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭu tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷic cārnta (Proto Dravididan race) toṭakka kālap paṇpāṭu eṉṟu muṭipu kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1 cintu veḷiyi āynta car cāṇ mārcal tam cīriya nūlil ivvūrik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.2
itaṉit toṭarntu 1899 mutal 1905 vari ceṉṉi aracāṅkat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi mēṟpārviyāḷarum akaḻ āyvuttuṟi atikāriyumāṉa aleccāntar irī (Alexander Rea F.S.A. Scot), avarkaḷiṉ ariya muyaṟciyāl ippakutiyi akaḻntu, aṅkuk kiṭitta poruḷkaḷ, ceṉṉit tol poruḷ kāṭcicāliyil, paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ tokutiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa paḻam poruḷkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻikkuppiṉ eḻunta pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ mūlatti uṭiyatu eṉṟu avar karutiṉār. āṉāl pērāciriyar ji. eliyaṭcimit, kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattiṟku muṉ - paṉṉūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku - muṉ paḻam pāṇṭiyar kālattil ippakutikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirukka lām eṉṟu karutukiṟār.
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṇṭi ōṭukaḷil pala, tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭi ōṭukaḷ eṉṟum, oṉṟu āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭu eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.3 eṉavē kiṟittava ūḻi arumpu muṉṉarē tirā viṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya pūmarāṅ eṉṉum - pakivarkaḷ mītu eṟintāl, avarkaḷit tākkiviṭṭu vīciyavarkaḷ kikku - varum oru vaki āyutam tamiḻakattil paṇṭu toṭṭuk kiṭippatum oru cāṉṟākak kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
ātitta nallūr nākarikam arappā nākarikattōṭu caṅkilit toṭarpōl oṟṟumiyuṭiyatāyt tikaḻkiṟatu eṉṟu cintuveḷi akaḻ āyviṉit tiṟampaṭa naṭattiya ār.ṭi.pāṉarci1 kūṟukiṟār.
kiṟittava ūḻikkumuṉ koṟkikku varum kappalkaḷ taṇporuni āṟṟiṉ mūlam ātittanallūr vari vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu intiya varalāṟṟut tuṟip pērāciriyar tiru pālakiruṭṭiṇa nāyar2 karutukiṟār.
ivikaḷiṉ mūlam cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tamiḻakattil āṟṟaṅkarip paṇpāṭum, payirttoḻil vaḷarcci yāl peṟṟa nakara nākarikamum, kāḷic ciṉṉamum koṟki, ātitta nallūr, nallūr nākarikaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷi aṭuttē maturi, pūmpukār, kāñci, vañci mutaliya āṟṟaṅkarip paṇpāṭu cintu āṟṟaṅkariyil malarnta mokañcatārō vari miḷirkiṉṟatu.
āriyarkaḷ intiya nāṭṭiṉuḷ aṭi eṭuttu vikkum poḻutu, intiyāvil uyariya tirāviṭa nākarikam nilavi niṉṟatu. attirāviṭa nākarikam ekiptu, mecapottāmiyā ākiya veḷi nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta nākarikam eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu aṟiñar palar tirāviṭa nākarikam intiyāvilē arumpi ekiptu papilōṉ, ellam, cumēriyā, acīriyā, akkēṭiyā kirīṭ nāṭu kaḷil paraviyatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.3
makkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, uṭal amippu, elumpukaḷiṉ taṉmi ākiyavikaḷi vittu makkaḷiṉam iṉṟu pirikkappaṭukiṟatu. mokañcatārō, arappā, paluccicut tāṉattil uḷḷa nāl (Nal), ātittanallūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntukaṇṭa elumpukkūṭukaḷ, paṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷiṉiṉṟum varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉ vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭi ōṭṭup puṭippukaḷāl uḷaviyal kūṟukaḷi viḷakkamāka aṟiyamuṭiyumeṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭu aṭip paṭiyil maṇṭiōṭṭu amippiyal āyvuttuṟiyiṉ mūlam aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ yār eṉṟu muṭipukaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ātittanallūril vāḻnta makkaḷum, arappāvil vāḻnta makkaḷum kicu (Kish) nakaril vāḻnta makkaḷum āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷavarkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ maṇṭi ōṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu āyntu kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. eliyaṭ cumit eṉṟa aṟiñariṉ āyviṉ paṭi ātittanallūril uḷḷa maṇṭiyōṭu paḻaṅkāla ekittiya makkaḷiṉ maṇṭi yōṭṭiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭatākap pirittuṇara muṭiyātatāka irukkiṟatu. villum, atti marattiṉ vaḻipāṭṭu marapum - oru niṟivāṉa camayak kōṭpāṭṭumuṟi, āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ kāṭarkaḷ teṉintiyak kāṭukaḷil uḷḷa ūrāḷikaḷiṭa miruntu peṟṟa naṉkoṭikaḷāka aṟiñarkaḷāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷap paṭṭa uṇmikaḷākum. pāṉi caṭṭikaḷum avarkaḷ aḷitta naṉ koṭikaḷēyākum. pūmarāṅ, teṉintiya ūtu kaṇikkuḻal (Blow gun) kumukāyap poṟuppuk kaṭṭuppāṭu ākiyavikaḷik kulamarapuc ciṉṉa aṭippaṭiyil koḷḷum vāḻkki muṟi (Totemism)í« avarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟṟavikaḷē eṉṟu mīṇṭum pativu ceytu koḷḷalām.1 aṭuttatāka, naṭunilikkaṭal cārnta makkaḷi eṭuttuk koḷḷalām potuvāka intiya makkaḷuṭiya - ciṟappākat teṉ intiya makkaḷuṭiya uṭal ākka amippum (Physical composition) kaṭalil kala mōṭṭum tiṟaṉum, payirt toḻiṟ kaliyum matippuyarnta teṉ intiyap paṇpāṭum kiḻakku naṭunilak kaṭaṟ pakutiyiṉiṉṟu vantatākak koḷḷalām. teṉ intiya intuk kaḷiṉ maṇṭiyōṭu armīṉiya nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ maṇṭi yōṭṭi ottateṉṟu tarcuṭaṉ teṉ intiyāviṉ kulappirivu kaḷum, iṉamarapuk kuḻukkaḷum eṉṟa nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.2 tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyē armīṉiya naṭunilak kaṭaṟpakuti makkaḷiṉ iṇippiṉ polivu peritum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu aṟiñar palarum kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
purucupuṭ eṉṉum aṟiñar āyvil, ātitta nallūril 1140-ēkkar aḷaviṟku mēl uḷḷa nilattil kāṇappaṭum putikuḻikaḷil putiya kaṟkālatti aṭuttu arumpiya paḻiya irumpuk kāla nākarikam nilaviyatu eṉṟu muṭipu kaṭṭiyuḷḷār. ātittanallūril arumpiya tamiḻar nākarikam arappā nākarikattōṭum cumēriya, ellam, ekittu, papilōṉiya aciriya, nākarikaṅkaḷōṭu caṅkilit toṭar pōl iṇintatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl muṭipu kaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
ātittanallūril akaḻntukaṇṭa, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya poruḷkaḷiyum tamiḻakattil uḷḷa nelli, maturi, irāmanātapuram, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntu kaṇṭa putiya kaṟkāla, paḻaṅkaṟkāla, tol paḻaṅkaṟkālak karuvikaḷiyum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal nilaṅkaḷiṉ nākarikaṅkaḷōṭu oppunōkki āyntu ekittiya cumēriya aciriya arappaṉ nākarikaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭum pārtta aṟiñarkaḷ tirāviṭa nākarikamē toṉmiyāṉatu; ciṟappāṉatu; itiṉiṉṟu ulakil, pala nākarikaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. immakkaḷē ulaka nākarikattiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ eṉṟum tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaḷē ekittilum ellattilum papilōṉilum kuṭiyēṟi irukka lām eṉṟum karutukiṉṟaṉar.
ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭu koṟkip paṇpāṭ ṭōṭu iṇintatu; arappā paṇpāṭṭōṭu neruṅkiya toṭar puṭiyatu.
aṟiñarkaḷ ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭi āḻntu aḻuttamāka ārāycci ceyya muṟpaṭṭāl potuvākat tamiḻar nākarikattip paṟṟiyum ciṟappākak koṟki nākarikattip paṟṟiyum pala ariya - putiya uṇmikaḷ veḷivarum.
ātittanallūril arumpiya ariya paṇpāṭṭiṉ ciṉṉaṅ kaḷum kōnakar koṟkiyil koḻuntuviṭṭat tuḷirtteḻunta nakara - āṟṟaṅkarip paṇpāṭum arappāvil aṟiñarkaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭa nākarikattōṭu ottiruppatu āḻntu cintittup pārppataṟ kuriyatu. nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē uvariyip paṟṟi naṭu nilak kaṭaṟ pakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ naṉkaṟintirutṉar. cālamōṉ ñāṉi virumpi vēṇṭiyamiyāl, el eṉṉum tēvaṉukkuk kaṭṭiya eḻil mikunta kōyilukku akil kaṭṭiyum mayil iṟakum āṭṭu mayirum, kuraṅkum piṟavum pīṉiciyar tam kappalil vantu ēṟṟic ceṉṟuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu viviliyat tiru nūlil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. uvari, koṟkikkut teṟkil uḷḷa paḻiya tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉamākum.

17. muṭivuri
aṟam ārntu aṇipeṟṟeḻunta arappāvum mokañca tārōvum āṭavalāṉum koṟṟaviyum teṉmukak kaṭavuḷum tiruviḷiyāṭal purinta tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ tiruviṭamākiya tirāviṭa intiyāviṟku iru kaṇkaḷ pōl viḷaṅkiṉa. tirāviṭa nākarikat tiṟku uyarnta eṭuttukkāṭṭāy ilaṅkiya iru perum nakaraṅkaḷum āriya arakkarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ nakara nākarikamum, avarkaḷatu ariya kāḷic ciṉṉamum, kirāma nākarika mum, pacuc ciṉṉamum koṇṭa āriyarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. ēṉ? ulakiṟkē oru nantā viḷakkeṉa oḷiviṭṭōṅkiya cintu veḷit tirāviṭa nākarikamum tirāviṭap paṇpum tirāviṭa āṭciyum 4500 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aṭiyuṭaṉ vīḻntaṉa.
appāl ataṉ aṭivēril kiḷitteḻunta tamiḻar āṭci yum avarkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷum mokalāyarkaḷāl vīḻttappaṭṭaṉa. tirāviṭarkaḷum, avarkaḷ vaḻivanta tamiḻarkaḷum vīram aṟṟa varkaḷ eṉṟō paṭipalam illātavarkaḷ eṉṟō araciyal aṟivu peṟātavarkaḷ eṉṟō evarum kūṟamuṭiyātu. āṉāl avarkaḷ āṭci ēṉ aṭiyōṭu cāyntatu? paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum avarkaḷ āṭci ēṉ talitūkkavilli? aḻintu pōṉa avarkaḷatu nakaraṅkaḷ ēṉ mīṇṭum talitūkki eḻavilli? ivi namakkup putirāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ituvari namatu nāṭṭu āṉṟōr kaḷum cāṉṟōrkaḷum aṟavōrkaḷum itippaṟṟi āya muṉ varavilli; uṇmi kaṇṭu namakku vaḻikāṭṭa villi. ataṉāl āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻar kumukāyam viḻippuṟṟu eḻavilli. taṅkaḷ vīḻccikku uriya kāraṇaṅkaḷi aṟintu tuyil kaḷintu mīṇṭu uyarvataṟkup pōrkkōlam pūṇṭeḻavilli.
cintu veḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷ payirttoḻil ceytu aṟa vaḻiyil vāḻntārkaḷ; paṇpu niṟintu niṉṟārkaḷ; nākarika vāḻvu naṭattivantaṉar; teyvattip pēṇi vāḻntaṉar. āṉāl aṉṉiya nāṭṭiṉar ayal iṉattavarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ vāḻvik kaṇṭu poṟāmi koṇṭu avarkaḷ aritiṉ muyaṉṟu kaṭṭiya nakaraṅ kaḷiyum viḷi nilaṅkaḷiyum, paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum cūṟiyāṭi aḻittu aṭimiyākka varuvārkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷ taṟpātukāp piṟkāṉa palamāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷ etuvum ceyyavilli. ākkiramikka varum ayalavarkaḷiṉ paṭikaḷi aḻittu avarkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam vari viraṭṭi avarkaḷ nāṭṭit tākki avar kaḷuṭiya paṭi kaḷiyum, paṭikkaruvikaḷiyum aḻittu aṟivūṭṭu vataṟku ēṟṟa, tākkum paṭikaḷi amikkavilli, ataṉāl tirāviṭa intiyā vīḻntatu. cintuveḷi nākarikattiṟku aṭip paṭiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutum tamiḻar nākarikamum kōnakar koṟkiyum kūṭak kālavuttāl tāḻntatu; ayalavar paṭi yeṭuppāl, ākkira mippāl nintatu; iyaṟkiyiṉ kōpattāl vīḻntatu.
arappāviṉ vīḻccikkuppiṉ tuḷirtta tamiḻnāṭum tamiḻ makkaḷum paṇṭiya cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷ pōl celvamum, nākarikamum aṟamum, neṟiyum, paṇpum oḻukkamum vāyntavarkaḷ. pakivarkaḷit tākki viraṭṭi avarkaḷ nāṭuvarip pōntu aṭittu vīḻtti avarkaḷukkum putti pukaṭṭum tiṟami mikkavarkaḷ eṉṟālum cērar eṉṟum, cōḻar eṉṟum pāṇṭiyar eṉṟum mūṉṟu nāṭākap pirintatum mūvarum oruvarukkoruvar aṭikkaṭi pōriṭṭu cakōtara nāṭukaḷiyum araṇmaṉikaḷiyum vīṭukaḷi yum, viḷi nilaṅkaḷiyum kalikaḷiyum, kalik karuvikaḷiyum aḻittu mūvaracarkaḷum palaṅkuṉṟi niṉṟamiyāl mūvarum aṭiyōṭu vīḻa nērntatu. paḻam perum pāṇṭiyar āṭci koḷḷiyaṉ camālutīṉ kiyāl eḷitil vīḻnta nilikaṇṭu pāṇṭiyakulam maṭṭumaṉṟi pāṇṭiyanāṭṭu makkaḷum, tamiḻ makkaḷum - ēṉ tirāviṭa iṉamum kaṇṇīr viṭātirukka muṭiyātu. pāṇṭiyanāṭu ellāt tamiḻarkaḷukkum tāyakam. avarkaḷ āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē cintuveḷi makkaḷōṭum, cīṉarkaḷōṭum cumēriyar kaḷōṭum ekittiyarkaḷōṭum vaṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭavarkaḷ. iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē āḻkaṭalkaḷik kaṭantu cellum kalaṅkaḷiyum kappaṟpaṭikaḷiyum kaṇṭavarkaḷ. imayam mutal īḻam vari ceṉṟu veṟṟiyīṭṭi imayattiṉ ucci yil kuṭiyi nāṭṭiyavarkaḷ īḻam vari pōntu tirukkōṇa mali mītu koṭiyip poṟittavarkaḷ; taṅkaḷatu valimi mikka kaṭaṟpaṭiyiṉ tiṟattāl ciṅkaḷam, parmā, antamāṉ, nikkōpār, kaṭāram, maliyam, ciṅkapuram, cāvakam, pālittīvu, tāylāntu, kampōcakam pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa nāṭukaḷi veṉṟu veṟṟik koṭiyip paṟakka viṭṭavarkaḷ.
yavaṉarkaḷōṭum kirēkkarkaḷōṭum urōmarkaḷōṭum arapiyarkaḷōṭum cumēriyarkaḷōṭum, ekittiyarkaḷōṭum cīṉarkaḷōṭum vaṇikam naṭattiyavarkaḷ; āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē kappaṟpaṭiyiṉ tiṟattāl kaṭal kaṭantu yāṇṭum veṟṟi yīṭṭi, camayattiyum kaliyiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum ñālamel lām nākarikattiyum parappiyavarkaḷ.
paḻamperum paṇpu vāynta, aṟaneṟi kaṇṭa tamiḻ makkaḷ mīṇṭun talitūkkit taḻittōṅka vēṇṭum. eṉavē, avarkaḷ mutalil tāṅkaḷ paṇpu vāynta nākarika makkaḷ eṉṟu uṇara vēṇṭum. appāl avarkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuḷ eḻunta iṉavēṟṟumi, kulavēṟṟumi, camaya vēṟṟumi aṉittiyum maṟantu oṉṟupaṭa vēṇṭum. appāl avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uyarvukkum urimikkum pōriṭa vēṇṭum. nāṭu nalampeṟa - tirāviṭa makkaḷ vāḻvu malara - aṟivūṭṭuka kiḷarnteḻuka caṭṭam ceyka eṉṉum poṉ moḻiyi namatu mūla mantiramākak koṇṭu eḻuvārkaḷāka.

paṭaṅkaḷiṉ viḷakkam
1. pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉum paṭṭattaraciyum
ippaṭam tirucci māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa putukkōṭṭikku aṇmiyil uḷḷa aṇṇal vāyil aṟivaṉ kōyil mukamaṇṭa pattiṉ muṉṉuḷḷa tūṇil tīṭṭap peṟṟa oru oppaṟṟa eḻil ōviyamākum inta ōviyam pāṇṭiyaṉ avaṉīpa cēkara cīvallapaṉum avaṉatu araciyum ki.pi. (835-862) araca uṭiyuṭaṉ aḻaku poliya niṟpatu pōl eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu - itu ki.pi. 9ām āṇṭil eḻutappeṟṟa neyvaṇṇa ōviyappaṭiyākum. ciliyiviṭa ōviyam aḻakuṭaṉ oḷirkiṟatu.
2. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭuk koṭi
iraṇṭām caṭavarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ I ilaṅkiyi veṉṟa niṉivukkuṟiyāka, avaṉāl tīṭṭap peṟṟa cācaṉattil caṭavarmaṉ vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ III”, īḻatti veṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa aracarkaḷil oruvaṉik koṉṟu avarkaḷuṭiya paṭikaḷic ciṟiyiṭṭu aracaṉ ēṟiccellum vaṇṭi, karuvūlam, aracu kaṭṭil, makuṭam, māli pōṉṟa aracac ciṉṉaṅkaḷik kippaṟṟip pāṇṭiyaṉatu iru mīṉkaḷ tīṭṭiya koṭiyit tirukkōṇa maliyil poṟittāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. antak koṭiyiṉ uruvamē iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum paṭamākum. tirukkōṇa maliyi ṉiṉṟu ikkoṭi tīṭṭappaṭṭa malippakuti uṭikkappaṭṭu ulāntāk kārarkaḷ kaṭṭiya ceykaṇṭa piraṭarik kōṭṭiyiṉ mītu vittuk kaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. (A.R.E.No. 356 of 1906 : 1-b. 19128 & 66) iṉṟu akkōṭṭi mītē koṭi uruvam kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.
3. pāṇṭiya aracu muttiri
ukkiraṉ kōṭṭic cācaṉattil poṟikkappaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ ciriparāntakaṉ vīranārāyaṇaṉāl (ki.pi. 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) tīṭṭap peṟṟa aracu muttiri. muttiriyiṉ naṭuvil kīḻē villum mēlē ceṅkuttāy niṟkum niliyil iru mīṉkaḷum, arukē uṭkārntirukkum puliyum tīṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu cintikkat takkatu. cōḻar aracu ciṉṉattilum ivvāṟē mūṉṟu aracukaḷiṉ ciṉṉamum iṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu.
4. pāṇṭiya iḷavaracaṉatu muttiri
inta muttiri pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu aracu muttiriyaṉṟu. itu pāṇṭiya iḷavaracaṉ apirāma ativīra rāmapāṇṭiyaṉiṉ conta muttiri (ilāñcaṉam) ākum. inta iḷavaracaṉ caṭilavarmaṉ cīvallapaṉ eṉṟa ati vīrarāmapāṇṭiyaṉ makaṉ āvaṉ. ivar ki.pi. 1593-ām āṇṭu irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa iru ciṟṟūrkaḷi pārppaṉarkaḷukkut tāṉamāka aḷittāṉ. ataṟkāka avaṉāl eḻuti vikkappaṭṭa ceppēṭṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa muttiriyē iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum muttiriyākum. intac ceppēṭu ceṉṉit tol poruḷ kāṭcic cāliyil uḷḷatu. (V.R. ii Ramnad 66 C.P.I of 1912).
5. aḵkacāli vināyakar kōyil
koṟkiyil (maturōtaya nallūril) uḷḷa oru kuḷakkari yil kāṇappaṭum kōyil aḵkacāli vināyakar kōyil eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itaṉic civaṉ kōyil eṉṟum itu cōḻar pāṇiyil paḻaṅkālac cōḻarkaḷāl kaṭṭappeṟṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ik kōyiliṉ vimāṉam mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil uḷḷa cōḻarkaḷiṉ vimāṉa amippip pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar pāṇṭi yarkaḷ kaṭṭiya intac civaṉ kōyili iṭikkālattil cōḻarkaḷ māṟṟiyamit tirukkalām eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ cuvarkaḷil kalveṭṭukkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. atil aḵkacāli īcuvaramuṭiyār kōyil eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
6. camaṇa tīrttaṅkarar cili
koṟki ūri aṭintatum vaḻiyil mutaṉ mutalākak kāṇappaṭuvatu intac camaṇamuṉivariṉ ciliyē. itu koṟki yil uḷḷa paḻaṅkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. maṟṟoru tīrttaṅkarar cili ūrukku aṇmiyil uḷḷatu tōṭṭam oṉṟil uḷḷatu. iṅku kiṟittava ūḻiyi oṭṭi camaṇaneṟi celvākkup peṟ ṟiruntatu eṉṟu eṇṇap paṭukiṟatu. iṅkup pala camaṇa camayatta varkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇuvatil viyappilli.
7.(m) paḻam pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷ
iṅkup paḻam pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ purāṇak kācukaḷum erutu uruvam poṟikkappaṭṭa kācukaḷum, yāṉi uruvam poṟikkap paṭṭa kācukaḷum piṟkālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. aṉittum cempiṉāl āṉavi. ivi koṟki aḵkacāliyil acciṭappaṭṭavi.
8. (ā) iṭikālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷ
ivi iṭikkālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷāka eṇṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. cilavaṟṟil cu eṉṟa tamiḻ varivaṭivam tīṭṭap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟil karuṭaṉ, kaṇṭa, kōṭari, civaṉ-pārvati uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi koṟkiyil acciṭap paṭṭavi.
9.(ï) iṟutik kālap pāṇṭiyar kācukaḷ
itil kāṇappaṭum kācukaḷ pāṇṭiya araciṉ iṟutikkālat til koṟki aḵkacāliyil acciṭap paṭṭavi. ivikaḷil karuṭaṉ, tirumāl, iḷampiṟi kaṇṭa kōṭari, viṇavarkaḷiṉ ciṉṉamākiya nāmam, caṅku, cakkaram mutaliyavi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
10. ātitta nallūr pāṉi caṭṭikaḷ
iṅkuḷḷa pāṉi caṭṭikaḷ kaṟuppu - civappu vaṇṇam vāyntavikaḷāy uḷḷaṉa. ivi palvēṟu vakikaḷāyk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi paṇṭikkālat tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ki vaṇṇatti eṭuttukkāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
11. ātittanallūr eḵku āyutaṅkaḷ
iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum eḵkuk karuvikaḷ ki.mu. 100 mutal 
ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukku uṭpaṭṭa kālattavi eṉṟu karutappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itil tiricūlam, vāḷ, īṭṭi, eḵku, ampumuṉikaḷ, uḷi, irupuṟamum viḷimpuḷḷa vāḷ, mīṉeṟivēl, eṟivēl pōṉṟavi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
12. ātittanallūr veṇkalap poruṭkaḷ
veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya vēlip pāṭukaḷ niṟinta pūntoṭṭikaḷum, cāṭikaḷum, kiṇṇaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itil pala ceṭikaḷum, koṭikaḷum, pūkkaḷum, arumpu kaḷum, ilikaḷum, vilaṅkukaḷum, paṟavikaḷum aḻakuṟac ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakattil veṇkalak kālamum irumpuk kālamum taḷirttuviṭṭaṉa eṉpa taṟku ivi ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāy miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.

Strabo says that he saw in 25 B.C. about 120 ships siling from Hormus to India (Merindle, Ane Ind P. 6) Embasies want him (Augustus empror of Rome) from several Indian states, for Augustus himself says that Indian embasies came frequently (Warmington op. eit. P. 35) Warmington thinks Sera the Pandya and the Sola monorchs of the time each sent separate embasies (Warmington op. cih. P. 37). This led to the volume of India’s trade with Rome in the time of Augustus expanding to huge proportions.

1.	“The evidence furnished by the Tamil classics of existence of an extensive chank-bangle industry in the extreme south of India during the height of ancient Tamil civilisation 1200 to 2000 years ago has received unexpected by conclusive corroboration within the present year (1912) through discoveries which I have made on the sites of the once famous Tamil cities of Korki and Kayal (now Palaya Kayal). These cities are now represented by mounds of rubbish adjacent tovillages stillbearing the appelation of their celebrated predecessors.
	The greatest find was at Korki which as already noted flourished from a date well antecedent to the christian era down to some indeterminate date prior to 1000 A.D. when the accretion of silt at the mouth of the Tambraparani drove the inhabitants to build another city (kayal) at the new mouth of the river. Here on the landward outskirt of the village I unearthed a five series of chank work shop waste, seventeen fragments in all. The whole numbers were found lying on the surface of the ground in a place where the old Pandyan coins have from time to time been discovered according to the information gathered in the village. “The Sacred chanks of India by James Hornell, F.L.S.” (1914) P. 45. Ibid P.46.

3.	The early writers of the church, however clim that the use of the fish to represent the Saviour was derived from a rebas taken from the Greek word for fish “ICHTHYS”, the letter of which are interpreted to mean Jesus Christ. God, Son, Saviour, One Calls Jesus “The Fisher of Men”; another says “Christ
	is figuratively called a fish”; another, “He is the fish that lives in the midst of water”, and still another “we small fish are like ICHTHYS, Our Jesus Christ born in water and saved only by remining in water” - Decorative Motives of Oriental Art by Katherine M. Ball. P. 201 (1927) London.
1.	The fish had already appeared in Pagan art, but the christians soon discovered, that in Greek language the five letters constituting the word ‘fish’ were the initial letter of the phrase “Jesus Christ the son of God the Saviour” writing about A.D.200. On Baptism Tertuleian sid “But we little fishes after the example of our fish Jesus Christ are born in water while Augustine (A.D.C. 425) sid that in the word fish” Christ is mystically understood becuse he was able to live that is, to exist without sin the abyss of this mortality as in the depth of waters”. Thus the fish might stand symbolically for the name of Christ. In the catacomb of Priscilla there is an inscription reading “Alexandar in” after which a fish is shown Completing the phrase Alexandar in Christ. Or fishes might stand for the Christians themselves. A grave inscription of Licinia Amias from around A.D. 200 (See illustration) has word “Fish” followed by “of the living” which must mean “Jesus christ, the son God the saviour of living” and is adorned with to fishes which may represent the christian who are “the living”. The inilials D.M. which appear on this inscription stand for Dis Manibas meaning “To the sprihits of the world of the dead”. These initials appeard regularly on Pagan graves and were friquetly employed by christians simplyas
	a conventional form and without further thought of their Original meaning” - Light from the ancient past - Jack Finegan (1951) PP. 382-383.

Of these princes Jatavarman Sundara Pandian 1, clims in his inscription to have conquered ceylon while Jatavarman Vira Pandia II, who took Elam A.D. 1253/4-1275) killed one of the two Kings, captured his army, charriots, treasures, throne, crown, necklace, bracelets, parasols, chures, and other royal possessions planted the Pandian flag with the double fish on Konamali and the high peaks of Trikutakiri mountin, received elephants as tribute from the other Kings of Ceylon subdued the Kerala* - (A.R.E.No 356 of 1906; ib 1912 & 65) - Ceylon coins and curreney - H.W.Codrington, B.A. (Oxon) C.C.S.; 1925.
2.	As sid above in the south also we have a Minavan the Pandyan King of Madura. His lanchanam was also the two fish may be seen in the Ramnad Plates of Abhirama Ativerarama Pandya in the Madras Museum in the Pandyan coins in all the temples built by the Pandyas in Southern India and even on the jambs
	of the gate of the Frederick Fort at Trincomalee in Ceylon. Agin he says that the horn fish of the flag of Greece is the same horn fish that was worshipped by the Pandya Minas Madura Kings” - The Minavan in Mohenjo - Daro - Rev. H. Heras, S.J.

1.	Physical features have been taken into account in determining racial elements. Prehistoric cronology can be studied from the finds at Adichanallur. Nal in Baluchistan and Mohenjo - Daro. It has been that the Mohenjo - Daro skulls of Proto - Australoid type are related to the skulls from Kish from Adichanallur and from those of the modern Veddhas. According to Elliot Smith an Adichanallur skull is indistinguishable from the early Egyptian type. The generally accepted theory is that the earliest inhabitants of the Peninsula were negroid in type akin to the Kadars and Uralis of South Indian forests and their contribution was probably the bow and the cult of the ficus tree - a fertility cult .... Next came the Mediteranian race. It is these that have been largely responsible for the physical composition of the peoples of Indian and especially South India and that have enriched South Indian culture Arts of agriculture & navigation came with them. These were from the eastern Mediteranean with them the Armenoids. - Origin and spread of the Tamils - Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1971. P. 6.

P. 15.
	The hypothesis that would represent what we know of their history most correctly would place their Original seat in the extreme South, some were probably not for from Madura or Tanjore and thence spreading fan-like towards the north. They have no traditions which point to any seat of race out of India or of their having emigrated from any Country whose inhabitants they can clim any kindred so far as they know they indigenous and oboriginal” - Tamilian Antiquary (No.1) - Dr. Fergusson.
	“Among the modern Indians, as amongst the modern Greeks or Italians the ancient Pre-Aryan type of the has (as the primitive of the alway does) survived, while that of the Aryan conqueror died out long ago and it is to this Dravidian ethnic type of India that the Sumerians bear most resemblance so far as we can judge from his monuments. He was very like a Southern Hindu of the Dekkan (who still speaks Dravidian languages) and it is by no means impossible that the Sumerians were an Indian race which passed certinly by land, perhaps also by sea, through Persia to the Valley of the two rivers. It was in the Indian home (perhaps Indus Valley) that their writing may have been invented and progressed from a purely pictorial to a simplified and abreviated form which afterwards in Babylonia took on its peculiat unifrom appearance owing to its being written within a aquare ended stilus on soft clay. On the way they left the seeds of culture in Elam ... ... there is little doubt that India must have been one of the earliest centres of civilization and it seems natural to suppose that the strange unsemitic uraryan people who came from the east to civilise the west were of Indian the Sumerians were in type” - An Ancient History of the Near East - Dr. R.Hall.
	The Madras presidency is the habitate of a Tamil Race whose civilization was the most ancient. A branch of Tamil Race spread a vast civilization on the Euphrates in very ancient times whose astrology, religion, love, morals and rites etc., furnished the foundation of the Asryian and Babylonian civilization and whose mythology was the source of the christian Bible. Another branch of them spread from the Malabar coast and gave rise to a wonderful Egyptian civilization and the Aryans are ir debted to the race in many respects. The colassal temples in South India proclim the Engineering Art of the Tamil race” - Qutoed in “Prabuddha Bharatha” (Monthly Magazine) by Swami Vivekananda Sep. 1921.
